# Agents · I · part 4

1,054 entries, implementer to implementer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **implementer**: Applies one well-specified change in an isolated worktree and commits it. Use when the main agent has already decided WHAT to change and the change touches files other parallel work does not. Give it the exact files, the acceptance test command, and the commit prefix. ([devhat-pm/Claude-Playbook](https://github.com/devhat-pm/Claude-Playbook/blob/HEAD/advanced/examples/multi-agent-orchestration/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Desarrollador senior que implementa features siguiendo planes de implementación. Usa este agente para ejecutar un plan creado por el planner o para implementar cambios de código. ([devlitus/travel-web](https://github.com/devlitus/travel-web/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **Implementer**: Implementa features siguiendo planes de implementación detallados. Escribe código, crea archivos y ejecuta comandos. ([devlitus/travel-web](https://github.com/devlitus/travel-web/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/implementer.agent.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Implements one bounded, fully specified unit of work, on sonnet by default and opus when dispatched with that override — a task group of an approved OpenSpec change, a tdd green step, a refactor move, or a map batch — with named paths, a failing test or named move, a verification command, and no judgment calls left. Does not plan, review, commit, tick tasks or ask the user; the main thread verifies and commits. Dispatched by /solo:execute, /solo:tdd, /solo:refactor and /solo:map only. ([diblaze/solo](https://github.com/diblaze/solo/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Implements exactly one task of a t4l feature document, test-first, inside the files declared for that task. Launched by the lead in the large lane after the specs are approved. Not for small changes. ([DiegoT4l/t4l-harness](https://github.com/DiegoT4l/t4l-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: 検証済みの計画どおりに実装して返す。implementation-flow のフェーズ 5 と、フェーズ 6・7 で採用した指摘の修正から呼ぶ。ゴールと計画を渡すと、コードとテストを書き、手元の検査を走らせて、変更したファイルと計画との差分を返す。git add / commit / push と Issue・PR への記録は行わない。 ([DIO0550/design-composer](https://github.com/DIO0550/design-composer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **implementer** ([dioscarr/glucoplate-ai](https://github.com/dioscarr/glucoplate-ai/blob/HEAD/.ai/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Kūkulu stage 2 — works through every open task in docs/PROGRESS.md unattended. Invoked by go-flight; do not delegate to it for ordinary coding. ([DisplaceTech/kukulu](https://github.com/DisplaceTech/kukulu/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Make one plan task's failing tests pass — smallest correct change, repo conventions, nothing beyond the task. ([dngioidev/forge](https://github.com/dngioidev/forge/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Implements an approved doggy-workflow spec without seeing its tests. Use only in that workflow's Implement + Test step, in parallel with test-implementer. ([Doggy-Footprint/doggy-harness](https://github.com/Doggy-Footprint/doggy-harness/blob/HEAD/.harness/agents/implementer.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **implementer**: AgentShip implementer. Executes one approved wave of a plan in the project, adds or updates tests for it, and reports what changed. Use only when /agentship:dev hands it a wave. ([dong4to/agentship](https://github.com/dong4to/agentship/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Implementation delegate that executes exactly one task node per dispatch for the example project and returns evidence against that node's acceptance criteria. ([DuBois-Company/agent-operations-template](https://github.com/DuBois-Company/agent-operations-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Low-effort implementer for a task with a statable acceptance criterion. Follows the neighbouring pattern under strict constraints, runs the harness, and returns a structured report instead of a guess. ([E1i/mikoshi-construct](https://github.com/E1i/mikoshi-construct/blob/HEAD/tests/fixtures/attach/record-v1/tree/.claude/agents/implementer.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **implementer**: Writes production code from a plan. Follows project conventions, never writes tests, and surfaces assumptions rather than guessing. ([eduardkumskyi/octo](https://github.com/eduardkumskyi/octo/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Implements a board task or an OpenSpec change group in a worktree — production code and its unit tests against an already-fixed contract (specs, scenario tests, task checklist). ([EduardoIllanes/ratchet](https://github.com/EduardoIllanes/ratchet/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Rust implementation specialist. ([EldosZhupanov/ising_engine](https://github.com/EldosZhupanov/ising_engine/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Implements one or a few tasks from specs/NNN-*/tasks.md against locked tests. Used by /sdd-implement. ([elvirkin1392/check-health](https://github.com/elvirkin1392/check-health/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Builds a change that has already been decided, such as a feature, a fix or a refactor, from a written spec or tech plan. It leaves the change working and tested but uncommitted. Use it once someone has said what should be built and why. Do not use it to decide what to build (spec-writer) or how it should look (ui-designer). ([emadbarandan/Arsenale-Ai-Agent-Company](https://github.com/emadbarandan/Arsenale-Ai-Agent-Company/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Junior engineer. Call for a bounded code, test, or documentation edit that has a check the child can run alone, typically after a senior wrote the interface, docstrings, and test list with the engineer skill. Fills bodies and tests inside the allowed paths, runs the check, returns the fixed report. Not for shared-contract changes, GPU jobs, git operations, or anything without a runnable check. ([emanuele-artioli/coding-agent-config](https://github.com/emanuele-artioli/coding-agent-config/blob/HEAD/archive/server-config-2026-09/.agent-rules/agents/implementer.agent.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Implements scoped changes with native TDD, bounded fix attempts, and revision-bound evidence. ([emirrtopaloglu/Taskard](https://github.com/emirrtopaloglu/Taskard/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Executes tasks.md one task at a time, test-first. Use for the /implement phase. Generates; never self-approves. ([EmmanuelRTM/sdd-claude-code-primitives](https://github.com/EmmanuelRTM/sdd-claude-code-primitives/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Owns one task end to end — plans it, writes it, tests it. The default for any subagent that will change code. Dispatched by orchestrate at Step 3, and by any skill handing off a whole unit of work. ([englishstreetventures/osn](https://github.com/englishstreetventures/osn/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: sharaqi only. Implements one bounded brief and runs its checks; the call sets model and effort. Never commits or spawns agents. ([EngSharaqi/sharaqi-workflow](https://github.com/EngSharaqi/sharaqi-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: TDD implementation worker for one scoped, well-specified task with acceptance criteria; tests first, then the minimal implementation, then green. Spawn it only when Erhan has authorized delegation for the task. ([erhanuenlue/tarifhub](https://github.com/erhanuenlue/tarifhub/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Implements a well-specified change (code and its tests) from a self-contained brief on Sonnet. Use for the build step of workflow size M and L slices after the orchestrator has settled the decisions, for fixing failing tests or review findings with a clear cause, and for mechanical multi-file edits. Not for open design questions. ([eSoul-cz/skills](https://github.com/eSoul-cz/skills/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)

([rvsree/hrb_chatbot_service](https://github.com/rvsree/hrb_chatbot_service/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md))
- **implementer**: Bounded coding worker. Use for one self-contained unit of implementation with a clear interface and a clear done condition, running in its own git worktree so parallel implementers never clash. Returns a summary of changes, not a transcript. &lt;example&gt; Context: The foreman plan has three independent work units user: "(plan approved) Build the migration, the repository method, and the API route" assistant: "I'll run three implementers in parallel worktrees, one per unit, and merge their branches when they report back." &lt;commentary&gt; Each unit touches different files and has a clean interface, so… ([RyanHelmn/foreman](https://github.com/RyanHelmn/foreman/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer.md))

En mode `observé` (pas de test automatisé), implémente selon les critères du ticket et prouve l'intégration (build/typecheck/lint/run). En mode `aucun` (spike), produit le code exploratoire sans exigence de test. Retourne l'état et les fichiers d'implémentation modifiés.
